### 5. 终端命令 · 退出码 0

```
/bin/zsh -lc "python3 -c \"
import json
with open('snapshot.json') as f:
    data = json.load(f)

# Current game info
c = data['current']
print('=== CURRENT GAME ===')
print(f'Event: {c[\\\"event\\\"]}')
print(f'Stage: {c[\\\"stage\\\"]}')
print(f'Series: {c[\\\"series\\\"]}')
print(f'Game n: {c[\\\"n\\\"]}')
print(f'Patch: {c[\\\"patch\\\"]}')
print(f'Blue: {c[\\\"blue\\\"]}, Red: {c[\\\"red\\\"]}')
print(f'Starters - Blue: {c[\\\"starters\\\"][\\\"blue\\\"]}')
print(f'Starters - Red: {c[\\\"starters\\\"][\\\"red\\\"]}')
print(f'Draft rule: {c[\\\"draft_rule\\\"]}')

# History games with patch 26.20 and relevant series
print('\\n=== HISTORY GAMES WITH PATCH 26.20 ===')
for g in data['history']:
    if g.get('patch') == '26.20':
        print(f'Series: {g[\\\"series\\\"]}, Game: {g[\\\"n\\\"]}, Blue: {g[\\\"blue\\\"]}, Red: {g[\\\"red\\\"]}, Winner: {g[\\\"blue_win\\\"]}, gold_gd15: {g[\\\"blue_gd15\\\"]}')

# Coverage info
print('\\n=== COVERAGE ===')
cov = data['coverage']
print(f'History games: {cov[\\\"history_games\\\"]}')
print(f'Starters with history: {cov[\\\"starters_with_history\\\"]}')
print(f'Coverage group: {cov[\\\"coverage_group\\\"]}')
print(f'Previous series games: {cov[\\\"previous_series_games\\\"]}')
print(f'Missing previous series games: {cov[\\\"missing_previous_series_games\\\"]}')
print('Starters coverage:')
for s in cov['starters']:
    print(f'  {s[\\\"side\\\"]} {s[\\\"name\\\"]}: {s[\\\"games\\\"]} games, {s[\\\"same_patch_games\\\"]} same patch')
\""
```

```
/bin/zsh -lc "python3 -c \"
import json
with open('snapshot.json') as f:
    data = json.load(f)

# Filter history games to patch 26.20, main event playoffs only (not play-in or round robin)
# But let's look at all games involving KCIE or T1 on 26.20
print('=== KARMININE CORP GAMES ON PATCH 26.20 ===')
for g in data['history']:
    if g.get('patch') == '26.20':
        blue_team = g['blue']
        red_team = g['red']
        winner = g['blue_win']
        gd15 = g['blue_gd15']
        series = g.get('series', 'N/A')
        stage = series.split('-')[-1] if series else 'N/A'
        # Check if KCIE is involved
        if 'Karmine' in blue_team or 'Karmine' in red_team:
            print(f'  {series} Game {g[\\\"n\\\"]}: {blue_team} (blue) vs {red_team} (red), blue_win={winner}, gd15={gd15}')

print()
print('=== T1 GAMES ON PATCH 26.20 ===')
for g in data['history']:
    if g.get('patch') == '26.20':
        blue_team = g['blue']
        red_team = g['red']
        winner = g['blue_win']
        if 'T1' in blue_team or 'T1' in red_team:
            print(f'  {g.get(\\\"series\\\", \\\"N/A\\\")} Game {g[\\\"n\\\"]}: {blue_team} (blue) vs {red_team} (red), blue_win={winner}, gd15={gd15}')

print()
print('=== SERIES Game 1 (for comparison) ===')
for g in data['history']:
    if g.get('series') == 'worlds-2026-main-event-quarterfinals-4' and g.get('n') == 1:
        print(f'  Game 1: {g[\\\"blue\\\"]} (blue) vs {g[\\\"red\\\"]} (red), blue_win={g[\\\"blue_win\\\"]}, gd15={g[\\\"blue_gd15\\\"]}')

print()
print('=== All quarterfinal series games on 26.20 ===')
for g in data['history']:
    if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and 'quarterfinals' in g.get('series', ''):
        print(f'  {g[\\\"series\\\"]} Game {g[\\\"n\\\"]}: {g[\\\"blue\\\"]} (blue) vs {g[\\\"red\\\"]} (red), blue_win={g[\\\"blue_win\\\"]}, gd15={g[\\\"blue_gd15\\\"]}')
\""
```

### 10. 终端命令 · 退出码 0

```
/bin/zsh -lc "python3 -c \"
import json
with open('snapshot.json') as f:
    data = json.load(f)

# Calculate blue win rate across all patch 26.20 games in history
total_games = 0
blue_wins = 0
for g in data['history']:
    if g.get('patch') == '26.20':
        total_games += 1
        if g.get('blue_win'):
            blue_wins += 1

print(f'Total patch 26.20 games: {total_games}')
print(f'Blue wins: {blue_wins}')
print(f'Blue win rate: {blue_wins/total_games if total_games > 0 else 0:.3f}')

# Now look at blue win rate in quarterfinals specifically
print()
print('=== Quarterfinals games on 26.20 ===')
qtr_games = [g for g in data['history'] if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and 'quarterfinals' in g.get('series', '')]
for g in qtr_games:
    print(f'  {g[\\\"series\\\"]} Game {g[\\\"n\\\"]}: {g[\\\"blue\\\"]} (blue) vs {g[\\\"red\\\"]} (red), blue_win={g[\\\"blue_win\\\"]}')

qtr_blue_wins = sum(1 for g in qtr_games if g.get('blue_win'))
qtr_total = len(qtr_games)
print(f'Quarterfinals blue win rate: {qtr_blue_wins}/{qtr_total} = {qtr_blue_wins/qtr_total if qtr_total > 0 else 0:.3f}')

# Look at main event games on 26.20
print()
print('=== Main event games on 26.20 ===')
main_games = [g for g in data['history'] if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and 'main-event' in g.get('series', '')]
for g in main_games:
    print(f'  {g[\\\"series\\\"]} Game {g[\\\"n\\\"]}: {g[\\\"blue\\\"]} (blue) vs {g[\\\"red\\\"]} (red), blue_win={g[\\\"blue_win\\\"]}, gd15={g.get(\\\"blue_gd15\\\")}')

main_blue_wins = sum(1 for g in main_games if g.get('blue_win'))
main_total = len(main_games)
print(f'Main event blue win rate: {main_blue_wins}/{main_total} = {main_blue_wins/main_total if main_total > 0 else 0:.3f}')

# Look at KCIE specifically
print()
print('=== KCIE games on 26.20 ===')
kcie_games = [g for g in data['history'] if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and ('Karmine' in g.get('blue', '') or 'Karmine' in g.get('red', ''))]
for g in kcie_games:
    print(f'  {g[\\\"series\\\"]} Game {g[\\\"n\\\"]}: {g[\\\"blue\\\"]} (blue) vs {g[\\\"red\\\"]} (red), blue_win={g[\\\"blue_win\\\"]}, gd15={g.get(\\\"blue_gd15\\\")}')

kcie_blue = [g for g in kcie_games if g['blue'] == 'Karmine Corp']
kcie_red = [g for g in kcie_games if g['red'] == 'Karmine Corp']
print(f'KCIE as blue: {len(kcie_blue)} games, {sum(1 for g in kcie_blue if g[\\\"blue_win\\\"])} wins')
print(f'KCIE as red: {len(kcie_red)} games, {sum(1 for g in kcie_red if g[\\\"blue_win\\\"])} wins (i.e., KCIE lost={sum(1 for g in kcie_red if not g[\\\"blue_win\\\"])} )')

# Look at T1 specifically
print()
print('=== T1 games on 26.20 ===')
t1_games = [g for g in data['history'] if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and ('T1' in g.get('blue', '') or 'T1' in g.get('red', ''))]
for g in t1_games:
    print(f'  {g[\\\"series\\\"]} Game {g[\\\"n\\\"]}: {g[\\\"blue\\\"]} (blue) vs {g[\\\"red\\\"]} (red), blue_win={g[\\\"blue_win\\\"]}, gd15={g.get(\\\"blue_gd15\\\")}')

t1_blue = [g for g in t1_games if g['blue'] == 'T1']
t1_red = [g for g in t1_games if g['red'] == 'T1']
print(f'T1 as blue: {len(t1_blue)} games, {sum(1 for g in t1_blue if g[\\\"blue_win\\\"])} wins')
print(f'T1 as red: {len(t1_red)} games, {sum(1 for g in t1_red if g[\\\"blue_win\\\"])} wins (T1 lost={sum(1 for g in t1_red if not g[\\\"blue_win\\\"])} )')
\""
```

```
/bin/zsh -lc "python3 -c \"
import json
with open('snapshot.json') as f:
    data = json.load(f)

# Analyze gd15 distribution for blue side on patch 26.20
gd15_values = []
for g in data['history']:
    if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and 'blue_gd15' in g:
        gd15_values.append(g['blue_gd15'])

print(f'Total gd15 values: {len(gd15_values)}')
print(f'Mean gd15: {sum(gd15_values)/len(gd15_values):.1f}')
print(f'Median gd15: {sorted(gd15_values)[len(gd15_values)//2]:.1f}')

# Count how many games blue was strictly ahead
ahead = sum(1 for g in gd15_values if g > 0)
behind = sum(1 for g in gd15_values if g < 0)
equal = sum(1 for g in gd15_values if g == 0)
print(f'Blue ahead (gd15 > 0): {ahead}/{len(gd15_values)} = {ahead/len(gd15_values):.3f}')
print(f'Blue behind (gd15 < 0): {behind}/{len(gd15_values)} = {behind/len(gd15_values):.3f}')
print(f'Blue equal (gd15 = 0): {equal}/{len(gd15_values)} = {equal/len(gd15_values):.3f}')

# KCIE gd15 as blue and as red
print()
print('=== KCIE gd15 as blue ===')
kcie_blue = [g for g in data['history'] if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and g.get('blue') == 'Karmine Corp']
for g in kcie_blue:
    print(f'  {g[\\\"series\\\"]} Game {g[\\\"n\\\"]}: gd15={g[\\\"blue_gd15\\\"]}, blue_win={g[\\\"blue_win\\\"]}')

print()
print('=== KCIE gd15 as red ===')
kcie_red = [g for g in data['history'] if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and g.get('red') == 'Karmine Corp']
for g in kcie_red:
    # gd15 is blue - red, so for KCIE (red), being ahead means gd15 < 0
    kcie_ahead = g['blue_gd15'] < 0  # negative gd15 means red (KCIE) ahead
    print(f'  {g[\\\"series\\\"]} Game {g[\\\"n\\\"]}: gd15={g[\\\"blue_gd15\\\"]}, KCIE ahead={kcie_ahead}, blue_win={g[\\\"blue_win\\\"]}')

# T1 gd15 as blue and as red
print()
print('=== T1 gd15 as blue ===')
t1_blue = [g for g in data['history'] if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and g.get('blue') == 'T1']
for g in t1_blue:
    print(f'  {g[\\\"series\\\"]} Game {g[\\\"n\\\"]}: gd15={g[\\\"blue_gd15\\\"]}, blue_win={g[\\\"blue_win\\\"]}')

print()
print('=== T1 gd15 as red ===')
t1_red = [g for g in data['history'] if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and g.get('red') == 'T1']
for g in t1_red:
    # gd15 is blue - red, so for T1 (red), being ahead means gd15 < 0
    t1_ahead = g['blue_gd15'] < 0
    print(f'  {g[\\\"series\\\"]} Game {g[\\\"n\\\"]}: gd15={g[\\\"blue_gd15\\\"]}, T1 ahead={t1_ahead}, blue_win={g[\\\"blue_win\\\"]}')

# Look at all quarterfinals gd15
print()
print('=== All quarterfinals gd15 ===')
qtr = [g for g in data['history'] if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and 'quarterfinals' in g.get('series', '')]
for g in qtr:
    print(f'  {g[\\\"series\\\"]} Game {g[\\\"n\\\"]}: {g[\\\"blue\\\"]} (blue) vs {g[\\\"red\\\"]} (red), gd15={g[\\\"blue_gd15\\\"]}, blue_win={g[\\\"blue_win\\\"]}')
\""
```

```
/bin/zsh -lc "python3 -c \"
import json
with open('snapshot.json') as f:
    data = json.load(f)

# Check correlation between being ahead at 15:00 and winning
total = 0
blue_ahead_and_blue_win = 0
blue_behind_and_blue_win = 0
for g in data['history']:
    if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and 'blue_gd15' in g:
        total += 1
        if g['blue_gd15'] > 0:
            if g['blue_win']:
                blue_ahead_and_blue_win += 1
        elif g['blue_gd15'] < 0:
            if g['blue_win']:
                blue_behind_and_blue_win += 1

print(f'Total games with gd15: {total}')
print(f'Blue ahead at 15 and blue wins: {blue_ahead_and_blue_win}')
print(f'Blue behind at 15 and blue wins: {blue_behind_and_blue_win}')
print(f'P(blue wins | blue ahead at 15): {blue_ahead_and_blue_win/45 if blue_ahead_and_blue_win else 0:.3f}')
print(f'P(blue wins | blue behind at 15): {blue_behind_and_blue_win/39 if blue_behind_and_blue_win else 0:.3f}')

# Also check for KCIE specific
print()
print('=== KCIE games: gd15 and outcome ===')
for g in data['history']:
    if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and (g.get('blue') == 'Karmine Corp' or g.get('red') == 'Karmine Corp'):
        gd15 = g.get('blue_gd15', 'N/A')
        bw = g.get('blue_win', 'N/A')
        kcie_is_blue = g['blue'] == 'Karmine Corp'
        kcie_ahead = gd15 > 0 if gd15 "'!= '"'N/A' else 'N/A'
        print(f'  {\\\"Blue\\\" if kcie_is_blue else \\\"Red\\\"} KCIE: gd15={gd15}, blue_win={bw}, KCIE ahead={kcie_ahead}')

# T1 specific
print()
print('=== T1 games: gd15 and outcome ===')
for g in data['history']:
    if g.get('patch') == '26.20' and (g.get('blue') == 'T1' or g.get('red') == 'T1'):
        gd15 = g.get('blue_gd15', 'N/A')
        bw = g.get('blue_win', 'N/A')
        t1_is_blue = g['blue'] == 'T1'
        t1_ahead = gd15 > 0 if gd15 "'!= '"'N/A' and gd15 "'!= 0 else False
        print(f'"'  {\\\"Blue\\\" if t1_is_blue else \\\"Red\\\"} T1: gd15={gd15}, blue_win={bw}, T1 ahead (gd15>0)={t1_ahead}')
\""
```

### 16. 模型消息

```
Now I have all the data I need. Let me summarize the key findings and make my estimates.

**Key Data Summary:**

| Metric | Value |
|---|---|
| Overall blue win rate on 26.20 (84 games) | 57.1% (48/84) |
| Blue ahead at 15:00 on 26.20 | 53.6% (45/84) |
| P(blue wins | blue ahead at 15) | 71.1% (32/45) |
| P(blue wins | blue behind at 15) | 41.0% (16/39) |
| KCIE as blue on 26.20 (12 games) | 9-3 (75%), 8/12 ahead at 15:00 |
| KCIE as blue main events (5 games) | 80% ahead at 15:00 (4/5) |
| T1 as blue on 26.20 (4 games) | 4-0 (100%) |
| T1 as red on 26.20 (2 games) | 1-1, 100% ahead at 15:00 (small sample) |
| Game 1 of series: T1 (blue) beat KCIE (red), gd15=-660 (KCIE ahead at 15:00 but T1 won) | |

**Analysis:**

- Game 1 of this series had T1 (blue) win vs KCIE (red) even though KCIE was ahead by 660 gold at 15:00 - T1 demonstrated strong late-game comeback ability.
- Fearless format means drafts are completely different in Game 2.
- KCIE as blue in main events on 26.20 has been ahead at 15:00 80% of the time (4/5 games).
- T1 as red was 100% ahead at 15:00 in their 2 games (very small sample).
- Overall blue win rate is 57.1%, but T1 is above-average quality while KCIE is variable.

**Estimates:**

- `p_blue_win`: T1 is the better team and won Game 1 as blue. While KCIE gets the blue side advantage in Game 2, T1's skill edge gives them the edge. I estimate KCIE (blue) wins about 45% of the time.

- `p_blue_ahead_15`: KCIE as blue in main events on 26.20 is 80% ahead at 15:00, well above the overall 53.6%. I'll adjust slightly downward due to T1's quality opponent factor.
```

### 23. 模型消息

````
The prediction file has been written successfully. Here's my analysis and final estimates:

## Analysis Summary

**Current game**: Game 2 of best-of-5, *worlds-2026-main-event-quarterfinals-4*, patch 26.20. Blue: Karmine Corp, Red: T1. Draft rule: Fearless (no carryover picks from Game 1).

**Key evidence considered**:

| Factor | Finding |
|---|---|
| Game 1 of same series | T1 (blue) beat KCIE (red) despite KCIE leading by 660 gold at 15:00 - T1 showed strong late-game comeback |
| Overall blue win rate (26.20, 84 games) | 57.1% (48/84) |
| Blue ahead at 15:00 (26.20) | 53.6% (45/84) |
| KCIE as blue (main events, 5 games) | 80% ahead at 15:00 (4/5), 75% win rate (9/12) |
| T1 as blue (26.20, 4 games) | 4-0 (100%), came back from gold deficit in 3 games |
| T1 as red (26.20, 2 games) | 1-1, 100% ahead at 15:00 (very small sample) |

**`p_blue_win` = 0.45**: T1 is the clear quality favorite having won Game 1 as blue and going 4-0 as blue on this patch. KCIE gets the blue side advantage in Game 2, but Fearless drafts means no carryover, and T1's superior skill edge prevails. KCIE's ability to come back from 15:00 gold deficits (as in Game 1) keeps the probability from being lower.

**`p_blue_ahead_15` = 0.55**: KCIE as blue in main events on 26.20 has been ahead at 15:00 80% of the time (4/5 games), far above the overall 53.6% baseline. I adjust slightly downward to 0.55 to account for T1 being a quality opponent who also tends to build leads when on red (100% in their 2 games).
````
